  "content": "import T from './transcripts.js';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Real data: every terminal line below comes from the real game and from git\n// (see transcripts.js). Nothing on a terminal is typed in by hand.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nconst V = T.victory;\nconst D = T.defeat;\nconst find = (lines, sub) => {\n  const l = lines.find((x) => x.includes(sub));\n  if (l === undefined) throw new Error(`transcript line not found: ${sub}`);\n  return l;\n};\nconst ASK = /^((?:Host|Waiter|Chef)\\s+\\[[^\\]]*\\]: |Happy Hour \\[[^\\]]*\\]: )(.*)$/;\n// A transcript line becomes a typed prompt (manager input) or plain output.\nconst item = (l, extra = {}) => {\n  const m = l.match(ASK);\n  if (m) return { ask: m[1], in: m[2], ...extra };\n  if (l.includes('! Not possible')) return { out: l, c: 'err', ...extra };\n  return { out: l, ...extra };\n};\nconst gap = () => ({ out: '  ...', c: 'muted' });\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Terminal: draws game output the way a terminal shows it, line by line, with\n// the manager's input typed after the prompt. Long lines wrap like a terminal.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nconst TC = { fg: '#e2e8f0', muted: '#94a3b8', prompt: '#34d399', input: '#fbbf24', err: '#fb7185', ok: '#86efac', hi: '#93c5fd' };\nconst HL = { hi: 'rgba(96,165,250,0.30)', new: 'rgba(52,211,153,0.30)', mod: 'rgba(251,191,36,0.28)', err: 'rgba(251,113,133,0.30)', ok: 'rgba(52,211,153,0.30)' };\nconst clamp = (v, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));\n\nfunction term(s, o) {\n  const size = o.size ?? 26;\n  const lh = size * 1.5;\n  const cw = size * 0.6;\n  const w = o.w ?? 1780;\n  const h = o.h ?? 850;\n  const x = o.x ?? s.cx;\n  const y = o.y ?? s.cy;\n  const pad = size * 0.9;\n  const bar = size * 1.5;\n  const cap = Math.max(1, Math.floor((h - bar - pad * 1.6) / lh));\n  const cols = Math.floor((w - pad * 2) / cw);\n  const cps = o.cps ?? 30;\n  const rows = [];\n  let clock = s.time(o.at ?? 0) + 0.5;\n\n  const wrap = (str) => {\n    if (str.length <= cols) return [str];\n    const out = [];\n    const indent = '    ';\n    let rest = str;\n    let first = true;\n    for (;;) {\n      const lim = first ? cols : cols - indent.length;\n      if (rest.length <= lim) break;\n      let cut = rest.lastIndexOf(' ', lim);\n      if (cut <= 0) cut = lim;\n      out.push((first ? '' : indent) + rest.slice(0, cut).trimEnd());\n      rest = rest.slice(cut).trimStart();\n      first = false;\n    }\n    out.push((first ? '' : indent) + rest);\n    return out;\n  };\n\n  const add = (it0) => {\n    const it = typeof it0 === 'string' ? { out: it0 } : it0;\n    if (it.block) {\n      it.block.forEach((b, i) => add({ ...(typeof b === 'string' ? { out: b } : b), c: (typeof b === 'string' ? it.c : b.c ?? it.c), at: i === 0 ? it.at : b.at }));\n      return;\n    }\n    const start = it.at !== undefined ? Math.max(clock, s.time(it.at)) : clock;\n    const hlT = it.hl === undefined || it.hl === false ? null : it.hl === true ? start : s.time(it.hl);\n    if (it.sh !== undefined || it.ask !== undefined) {\n      const isSh = it.sh !== undefined;\n      const text = isSh ? it.sh : it.in;\n      const rate = it.cps ?? cps;\n      const dur = text.length / rate;\n      rows.push({ kind: 'in', prompt: isSh ? '$ ' : it.ask, pcol: isSh ? TC.prompt : TC.muted, text, tcol: isSh ? TC.fg : TC.input, start, tstart: start + 0.2, rate, hlT, hlc: it.hlc ?? 'hi' });\n      if (text.length) s.sfx('type', start + 0.2, { dur, gain: 0.2 });\n      clock = start + 0.2 + dur + (it.after ?? 0.4);\n    } else if (it.pause) {\n      clock = start + it.pause;\n    } else {\n      for (const ln of wrap(String(it.out ?? ''))) rows.push({ kind: 'out', text: ln, col: TC[it.c] ?? TC.fg, start, hlT, hlc: it.hlc ?? 'hi' });\n      clock = start;\n    }\n  };\n  o.lines.forEach(add);\n\n  s.draw({ x, y, at: o.at ?? 0, out: o.out, enter: 'fade', outDur: 0.4 }, (ctx) => {\n    const t = s.t;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(15,23,42,0.30)';\n    ctx.shadowBlur = 32;\n    ctx.shadowOffsetY = 12;\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f172a';\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.roundRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 18);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.roundRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 18);\n    ctx.clip();\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';\n    ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, bar);\n    ['#ff5f57', '#febc2e', '#28c840'].forEach((c, i) => {\n      ctx.fillStyle = c;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(-w / 2 + pad * 0.8 + i * size * 0.8, -h / 2 + bar / 2, size * 0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.fill();\n    });\n    ctx.font = `400 ${size * 0.7}px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace`;\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#94a3b8';\n    ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n    ctx.fillText(o.title ?? 'restaurant-rush', 0, -h / 2 + bar / 2);\n    ctx.font = `400 ${size}px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace`;\n    ctx.textAlign = 'left';\n    ctx.textBaseline = 'top';\n    const vis = rows.filter((r) => t >= r.start);\n    const off = Math.max(0, vis.length - cap);\n    const top = -h / 2 + bar + pad * 0.8;\n    const left = -w / 2 + pad;\n    vis.slice(off).forEach((r, i) => {\n      const ry = top + i * lh;\n      if (r.hlT !== null && t >= r.hlT) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = HL[r.hlc] ?? HL.hi;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = clamp((t - r.hlT) / 0.3);\n        ctx.fillRect(-w / 2 + pad * 0.4, ry - lh * 0.12, w - pad * 0.8, lh);\n        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n      }\n      if (r.kind === 'out') {\n        ctx.fillStyle = r.col;\n        ctx.fillText(r.text, left, ry);\n      } else {\n        ctx.fillStyle = r.pcol;\n        ctx.fillText(r.prompt, left, ry);\n        const n = clamp(Math.floor((t - r.tstart) * r.rate), 0, r.text.length);\n        ctx.fillStyle = r.tcol;\n        ctx.fillText(r.text.slice(0, n), left + r.prompt.length * cw, ry);\n        const last = i === vis.length - off - 1;\n        if (last && (n < r.text.length || Math.floor(s.T * 2) % 2 === 0)) {\n          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(226,232,240,0.85)';\n          ctx.fillRect(left + (r.prompt.length + n) * cw + 2, ry + size * 0.08, cw * 0.85, size * 1.1);\n        }\n      }\n    });\n    ctx.restore();\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Diagrams: the real PDF diagrams of the repository, shown full page and then\n// zoomed in on the part the narration talks about. Captions and labels are\n// drawn on the screen (not zoomed) with hud().\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nconst DG = {\n  c1: { src: 'assets/stage-1-class.png', nw: 2377, nh: 3331, dw: 1427, dh: 2000 },\n  c2: { src: 'assets/stage-2-class.png', nw: 2799, nh: 3814, dw: 1468, dh: 2000 },\n  c3: { src: 'assets/stage-3-class.png', nw: 3399, nh: 4802, dw: 1416, dh: 2000 },\n  q1: { src: 'assets/stage-3-sequence-p1.png', nw: 3888, nh: 3406, dw: 2000, dh: 1752 },\n  q2: { src: 'assets/stage-3-sequence-p2.png', nw: 3888, nh: 3301, dw: 2000, dh: 1698 },\n  q3: { src: 'assets/stage-3-sequence-p3.png', nw: 3888, nh: 2676, dw: 2000, dh: 1376 },\n};\nconst dgeom = (s, key, h) => {\n  const d = DG[key];\n  const w = (h * d.nw) / d.nh;\n  return { d, x: s.cx, y: s.cy, w, h, k: w / d.dw };\n};\n// r = [u0, v0, u1, v1] in the pixel space of the diagram image as displayed at its page size\nconst view = (s, g, r, o = {}) => {\n  const [u0, v0, u1, v1] = r;\n  const rw = (u1 - u0) * g.k;\n  const rh = (v1 - v0) * g.k;\n  const area = o.area ?? { w: 1760, h: 780, cy: 500 };\n  const zoom = Math.min(o.maxZoom ?? 3.4, area.w / rw, area.h / rh);\n  const wx = g.x + ((u0 + u1) / 2 - g.d.dw / 2) * g.k;\n  const wy = g.y + ((v0 + v1) / 2 - g.d.dh / 2) * g.k;\n  return { at: o.at, x: wx, y: wy + (s.cy - area.cy) / zoom, zoom, dur: o.dur ?? 1.3 };\n};\nconst drawDiagram = (s, g) => s.image(g.d.src, { x: g.x, y: g.y, h: g.h, at: -1 });\n// Draw in screen coordinates while the camera is zoomed: (0, 0) is the screen centre.\nconst hud = (s, cam, fn) => s.group({ x: cam.x, y: cam.y, scale: 1 / cam.zoom }, fn);\nconst caption = (text, at, out, o = {}) => ({ text, at, out, ...o });\nconst captions = (s, list) =>\n  list.forEach((c) =>\n    s.text(c.text, { x: 0, y: c.y ?? 440, size: c.size ?? 40, bold: true, color: '#ffffff', bg: '#111827', padding: 22, radius: 14, maxWidth: 1640, lineHeight: 1.2, at: c.at, out: c.out, exit: 'fade', enter: 'fade', sfx: false }));\nconst tag = (s, text) => s.text(text, { x: -840, y: -474, align: 'left', size: 36, bold: true, color: 'accent', at: -1 });\n\n// A small label at the top left of normal scenes, so the viewer knows the section.\nconst sec = (s, label) => s.text(label, { x: 120, y: 62, align: 'left', size: 32, color: 'muted', at: -1 });\n\nconst money = (c) => `$${(c / 100).toFixed(2)}`;\nvoid money;\n\nexport default {\n  // ===================== 1. Game overview and the three stages =====================\n  title(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    s.icon('chef-hat', { x: s.cx, y: 270, size: 150, color: 'accent', enter: 'none', at: 0 });\n    s.title('Restaurant Rush', { y: 470, size: 150, enter: 'none' });\n    s.subtitle('SC2002 group project  ·  lab group SCEC, group 5', { y: 610, enter: 'none' });\n    const t0 = s.time('#java');\n    ['Java 17', 'Command line', 'Turn based'].forEach((label, i) => {\n      s.box(label, { x: s.cx + (i - 1) * 470, y: 810, w: 400, size: 46, color: ['blue', 'green', 'orange'][i], at: t0 + i * 0.5 });\n    });\n  },\n\n  manager(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Game overview');\n    s.text('You are the *manager*', { y: 190, size: 92, font: 'display', at: 0.4 });\n    const xs = s.row(4, { width: 1480 });\n    const staff = [\n      ['Waiter', 'hand-platter', 'blue', 'Waiter', 'seat, take order, serve'],\n      ['Chef', 'chef-hat', 'orange', 'Chef', 'cook one unit'],\n      ['Cashier', 'receipt', 'green', 'Cashier', 'one payment, automatic'],\n      ['Host', 'concierge-bell', 'purple', 'Host', 'Stage 3: seat one customer'],\n    ];\n    staff.forEach(([name, icon, color, cue, note], i) => {\n      s.box(name, { id: `st${i}`, x: xs[i], y: 470, w: 340, h: 130, size: 54, icon, color, at: cue });\n      s.text(note, { x: xs[i], y: 600, size: 34, color: 'muted', maxWidth: 340, at: '#limit' });\n    });\n    s.text('one task per turn', { x: s.cx - 370, y: 690, size: 44, color: 'accent', bold: true, at: '#limit' });\n    s.box('You decide who goes first', { x: s.cx, y: 840, size: 60, icon: 'scale', color: 'accent', at: '#choose' });\n  },\n\n  base(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Game overview');\n    s.text('The base setting is fixed', { y: 170, size: 80, font: 'display', at: 0.4 });\n    s.box('18 turns', { x: 620, y: 300, size: 50, color: 'blue', at: 'eighteen' });\n    s.box('2 tables', { x: 1300, y: 300, size: 50, color: 'orange', at: 'tables' });\n    const a = s.time('#arrivals');\n    const arrivals = [1, 4, 7, 10, 13, 16];\n    s.draw({ at: 1.2, enter: 'fade' }, (ctx) => {\n      const n = 18;\n      const x0 = -780;\n      const dx = 1560 / (n - 1);\n      ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n        const turn = i + 1;\n        const k = arrivals.indexOf(turn);\n        const p = k >= 0 ? clamp((s.t - (a + k * 0.32)) / 0.3) : 0;\n        const px = x0 + i * dx;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(px, 0, 36, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p > 0 ? `rgba(59,108,246,${0.15 + 0.85 * p})` : '#e5e9f2';\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.fillStyle = p > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#344054';\n        ctx.font = '700 34px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\", sans-serif';\n        ctx.fillText(String(turn), px, 2);\n        if (k >= 0) {\n          ctx.globalAlpha = p;\n          ctx.fillStyle = '#3b6cf6';\n          ctx.font = '600 30px \"Inter\", sans-serif';\n          ctx.fillText('customer', px, 78);\n          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n        }\n      }\n    });\n    // the draw is centred on the screen; move it down by wrapping in a group\n    s.box('At least 4\\npaid customers', { x: 440, y: 800, w: 460, h: 200, size: 46, icon: 'users', color: 'green', at: '#paid' });\n    s.box('At least $45.00\\nrevenue', { x: 960, y: 800, w: 460, h: 200, size: 46, icon: 'banknote', color: 'blue', at: '#rev' });\n    s.box('Average satisfaction\\nat least 60', { x: 1480, y: 800, w: 460, h: 200, size: 46, icon: 'smile', color: 'purple', at: '#sat' });\n  },\n\n  turn(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Game overview');\n    s.text('Six steps every turn', { x: 160, y: 170, size: 80, font: 'display', align: 'left', at: 0.4 });\n    s.list(\n      [\n        { text: 'Arrivals', at: '#s1' },\n        { text: 'Manager choices', at: '#s2' },\n        { text: 'Kitchen and serving', at: '#s3' },\n        { text: 'Eating and payment', at: '#s4' },\n        { text: 'Waiting and abandonment', at: '#s5' },\n        { text: 'Result after turn 18', at: '#s6' },\n      ],\n      { x: 190, y: 290, size: 58, gap: 34, bullet: 'number', width: 900 },\n    );\n    s.box('The Waiter acts before the Chef', { x: 1440, y: 360, w: 640, size: 42, icon: 'arrow-down-up', color: 'blue', at: '#later' });\n    s.box('Ready on turn n,\\nserved on turn n+1 at the earliest', { x: 1440, y: 590, w: 640, size: 42, icon: 'clock', color: 'orange', at: s.cue('serving') });\n    s.box('Eating and payment\\nalso need later turns', { x: 1440, y: 820, w: 640, size: 42, icon: 'utensils', color: 'green', at: s.cue('eating', 2) });\n  },\n\n  stages(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Game overview');\n    s.text('Three tagged stages, each runnable on its own', { y: 170, size: 70, font: 'display', at: 0.4 });\n    const xs = [430, 960, 1490];\n    const cards = [\n      ['Stage 1', 'Basic game', 'blue', 'stage-1', ['Regular customers', 'Menu and Table', 'Waiter, Chef, Cashier', 'Victory and defeat'], '#s1'],\n      ['Stage 2', 'Customer behaviour', 'green', 'stage-2', ['VIP customer', 'Critic customer', 'Same order flow'], '#s2'],\n      ['Stage 3', 'Role and menu policy', 'purple', 'stage-3', ['Host', 'ComboMeal', 'Happy Hour'], '#s3'],\n    ];\n    cards.forEach(([name, sub, color, gitTag, items, at], i) => {\n      s.box(`${name}\\n${sub}`, { x: xs[i], y: 400, w: 470, h: 190, size: 44, color, at });\n      s.text(items.join('\\n'), { x: xs[i], y: 640, size: 40, lineHeight: 1.45, valign: 'top', maxWidth: 460, at });\n      s.text(`git tag ${gitTag}`, { x: xs[i], y: 930, size: 36, font: 'mono', color, at });\n    });\n  },\n\n  // ===================== 2. Gameplay demonstration =====================\n  launch(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration');\n    const t1 = V.turns[1];\n    term(s, {\n      y: 560,\n      h: 880,\n      title: 'repository root',\n      lines: [\n        { sh: 'javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java', at: '#build', cps: 34 },\n        { sh: 'java -cp out restaurantrush.Main', at: '#run', cps: 34 },\n        { block: V.head },\n        '',\n        { block: t1.slice(0, 10), at: '#screen' },\n        item(t1[10]),\n      ],\n    });\n  },\n\n  choice(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: a real manager decision');\n    const t = V.turns[13];\n    term(s, {\n      y: 560,\n      h: 880,\n      title: 'victory run, turn 13',\n      out: s.time('#cards') - 0.3,\n      lines: [\n        { block: t.slice(0, 14) },\n        item(t[14], { at: '#host' }),\n        item(t[15]),\n        item(t[16], { at: '#serve', hl: true, hlc: 'ok' }),\n        item(t[17]),\n        item(t[18]),\n      ],\n    });\n    s.text('Same recorded run, one choice changed', { y: 170, size: 66, font: 'display', at: '#cards' });\n    s.box('Serve the Critic first', { x: 520, y: 330, w: 700, size: 50, icon: 'check', color: 'green', at: '#cards' });\n    s.text('Critic pays with satisfaction *54*\\nAverage satisfaction *81.2*\\nRevenue $83.70', { x: 520, y: 640, size: 50, lineHeight: 1.5, maxWidth: 700, at: s.time('#cards') + 1.4 });\n    s.box(\"Take customer 5's order first\", { x: 1400, y: 330, w: 700, size: 50, icon: 'x', color: 'orange', at: s.cue('If') });\n    s.text('Critic waits one more turn\\nCritic pays with satisfaction *42*\\nAverage satisfaction *78.8*\\nRevenue $83.70', { x: 1400, y: 680, size: 50, lineHeight: 1.5, maxWidth: 700, at: s.cue('pays', 2) });\n  },\n\n  invalid(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: invalid choices');\n    const t = V.turns[6];\n    term(s, {\n      y: 560,\n      h: 880,\n      title: 'victory run, turn 6',\n      lines: [\n        { block: t.slice(0, 11) },\n        item(t[11]),\n        item(t[12], { at: '#rej' }),\n        item(t[13], { hl: true, hlc: 'err' }),\n        item(t[14], { at: '#kept' }),\n        item(t[15], { hl: true, hlc: 'ok' }),\n        item(t[16]),\n      ],\n    });\n  },\n\n  lifecycle(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: from READY to SERVED to payment');\n    const steps = [\n      ['Turn 1', 'Chef: dish READY', 'orange', 'chef-hat', '#cook'],\n      ['Turn 2', 'Waiter: SERVED', 'blue', 'hand-platter', '#serve'],\n      ['Turn 3', 'Eating: READY_TO_PAY', 'purple', 'utensils', '#eat'],\n      ['Turn 4', 'Cashier: PAID $10.80', 'green', 'receipt', '#paid'],\n    ];\n    const xs = s.row(4, { width: 1500 });\n    steps.forEach(([turn, what, color, icon, at], i) => {\n      s.box(`${turn}\\n${what}`, { id: `lc${i}`, x: xs[i], y: 250, w: 380, h: 190, size: 38, icon, color, at });\n      if (i) s.arrow(`lc${i - 1}`, `lc${i}`, { at });\n    });\n    const lines = [\n      [1, 'Chef works on the Burger', '#cook'],\n      [2, 'Waiter serves the Burger', '#serve'],\n      [3, 'finishes eating', '#eat'],\n      [4, 'Cashier collects', '#paid'],\n    ];\n    const items = [];\n    lines.forEach(([n, sub, at]) => {\n      items.push({ out: `turn ${n}`, c: 'muted', at });\n      items.push({ out: find(V.turns[n], sub), hl: true, hlc: 'ok' });\n    });\n    term(s, { y: 740, h: 520, size: 28, title: 'victory run, customer #1 (VIP)', lines: items });\n  },\n\n  customers(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: Stage 2 customers');\n    s.text('Same flow, different behaviour', { y: 170, size: 76, font: 'display', at: 0.4 });\n    const xs = [440, 960, 1480];\n    s.box('Regular\\nloses 8 per turn\\nfull price', { x: xs[0], y: 470, w: 440, h: 330, size: 46, icon: 'user', color: 'blue', at: '#regular' });\n    s.box('VIP\\nloses 5 per turn\\n10% off the bill\\nBurger $13.50', { x: xs[1], y: 470, w: 440, h: 330, size: 46, icon: 'crown', color: 'purple', at: '#vip' });\n    s.box('Critic\\nloses 12 per turn\\nmost expensive dish', { x: xs[2], y: 470, w: 440, h: 330, size: 46, icon: 'star', color: 'red', at: '#critic' });\n    s.box('Loses 10 more if served more than 2 turns after ordering', { x: xs[2], y: 800, w: 520, size: 38, color: 'red', dashed: true, at: '#late' });\n  },\n\n  host(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: Stage 3, the Host');\n    const t = V.turns[1];\n    term(s, {\n      y: 560,\n      h: 880,\n      title: 'victory run, turn 1',\n      lines: [\n        { block: t.slice(7, 10) },\n        gap(),\n        item(t[15], { at: '#seat' }),\n        item(t[16], { hl: true, hlc: 'new' }),\n        item(t[17], { at: '#order' }),\n        item(t[18], { hl: true, hlc: 'new' }),\n        item(t[19], { at: '#chef' }),\n        item(t[20]),\n      ],\n    });\n  },\n\n  combo(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: Stage 3, combo meals');\n    s.text('Price = 90% of the two prices added.  Units = the two added.', { y: 170, size: 52, at: '#rule' });\n    const row = (y, a, b, res, at) => {\n      s.box(a, { id: `${at}a`, x: 330, y, w: 360, h: 150, size: 40, color: 'blue', at });\n      s.text('+', { x: 580, y, size: 80, color: 'muted', at });\n      s.box(b, { id: `${at}b`, x: 830, y, w: 360, h: 150, size: 40, color: 'blue', at });\n      s.arrow(`${at}b`, `${at}r`, { label: 'x 0.9', labelFont: 'body', labelSize: 40, at });\n      s.box(res, { id: `${at}r`, x: 1520, y, w: 520, h: 170, size: 46, color: 'green', at });\n    };\n    // result boxes are drawn first in layout order so that arrows can find them\n    row(430, 'Salad\\n$9.00 · 1 unit', 'Burger\\n$15.00 · 1 unit', 'Salad + Burger\\n$21.60 · 2 units', '#c1');\n    row(760, 'Salad\\n$9.00 · 1 unit', 'Pasta\\n$18.00 · 2 units', 'Salad + Pasta\\n$24.30 · 3 units', '#c2');\n  },\n\n  happy(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: Stage 3, Happy Hour');\n    const t1 = V.turns[1];\n    const t2 = V.turns[2];\n    const t3 = V.turns[3];\n    term(s, {\n      y: 400,\n      h: 640,\n      size: 24,\n      title: 'victory run, turns 1 to 3',\n      lines: [\n        item(t1[10], { at: '#on' }),\n        item(t1[11], { hl: true, hlc: 'ok' }),\n        item(t1[12], { at: '#lasts' }),\n        gap(),\n        item(t1[17], { at: '#disc' }),\n        item(t1[18], { hl: '#locked', hlc: 'ok' }),\n        gap(),\n        item(find(t2, 'Happy Hour: ACTIVE'), { at: '#recover' }),\n        item(find(t2, 'recover'), { hl: true, hlc: 'ok' }),\n        gap(),\n        item(find(t3, 'Happy Hour:'), { at: '#used', hl: true, hlc: 'hi' }),\n      ],\n    });\n    s.text('$15.00 x 0.9 x 0.8 = *$10.80*', { y: 840, size: 92, font: 'display', at: '#locked' });\n    s.text('VIP discount first, then Happy Hour. The bill is locked when the order is taken.', { y: 960, size: 36, color: 'muted', maxWidth: 1500, at: '#locked' });\n  },\n\n  victory(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: victory run');\n    const t = V.turns[18];\n    const custs = t.filter((l) => l.startsWith('  #'));\n    term(s, {\n      y: 560,\n      h: 880,\n      title: 'repository root',\n      lines: [\n        { sh: 'java -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/victory.txt', at: '#cmd', cps: 34 },\n        gap(),\n        { block: [t[1], t[5]], at: 0.8 },\n        ...custs.map((l, i) => ({ out: l, hl: i === 5 ? '#sixth' : false, hlc: 'mod' })),\n        gap(),\n        { block: V.over.map((l, i) => ({ out: l, c: i === 1 ? 'ok' : undefined, hl: i >= 2 && i <= 4 ? '#win' : false, hlc: 'ok' })), at: '#win' },\n      ],\n    });\n  },\n\n  defeat(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Gameplay demonstration: defeat run');\n    const t = D.turns[18];\n    const custs = t.filter((l) => l.startsWith('  #'));\n    term(s, {\n      y: 560,\n      h: 880,\n      title: 'repository root',\n      lines: [\n        { sh: 'java -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/defeat.txt', at: 0.8, cps: 34 },\n        gap(),\n        { block: [t[1], t[5]] },\n        ...custs.map((l, i) => ({ out: l, hl: i === 1 ? '#leave' : false, hlc: 'err' })),\n        { out: find(t, 'runs out of patience'), hl: '#leave', hlc: 'err', at: '#leave' },\n        gap(),\n        { block: D.over.map((l, i) => ({ out: l, c: i === 1 ? 'err' : undefined, hl: i >= 2 && i <= 4 ? '#lose' : false, hlc: 'err' })), at: '#lose' },\n      ],\n    });\n  },\n\n  // ===================== 3. Class diagrams and stage changes =====================\n  stage1class(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'c1', 1040);\n    const cam = s.camera([\n      view(s, g, [240, 30, 1000, 470], { at: '#layers' }),\n      view(s, g, [380, 780, 1170, 1330], { at: '#restaurant' }),\n      view(s, g, [0, 1390, 1090, 1990], { at: '#customer' }),\n    ]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Stage 1 class diagram');\n      captions(s, [\n        caption('Boundary, control and entity layers', 0.6, '#layers'),\n        caption('RestaurantCLI (boundary)  ·  GameController (control)', '#layers', '#restaurant'),\n        caption('Restaurant: composition of Tables and staff, aggregation of Customers', '#restaurant', '#customer'),\n        caption('Private state, guarded transitions: Customer and Order', '#customer', undefined),\n      ]);\n    });\n  },\n\n  evolution(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Class diagrams: the same style at every stage');\n    const H = 780;\n    const keys = ['c1', 'c2', 'c3'];\n    const widths = keys.map((k) => (H * DG[k].nw) / DG[k].nh);\n    const gapPx = 46;\n    const total = widths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + gapPx * 2;\n    let left = (1920 - total) / 2;\n    const ats = ['#s1', '#s2', '#s3'];\n    const labels = ['Stage 1: all new', 'Stage 2: new and changed', 'Stage 3: new and changed'];\n    keys.forEach((k, i) => {\n      const cx = left + widths[i] / 2;\n      left += widths[i] + gapPx;\n      s.image(DG[k].src, { x: cx, y: 560, h: H, at: ats[i] });\n      s.text(labels[i], { x: cx, y: 130, size: 42, bold: true, at: ats[i] });\n    });\n    s.box('new', { x: 640, y: 1010, w: 200, size: 34, color: 'green', at: '#s2' });\n    s.box('changed', { x: 960, y: 1010, w: 240, size: 34, color: 'yellow', at: '#s2' });\n    s.box('unchanged', { x: 1290, y: 1010, w: 260, size: 34, color: 'blue', at: '#s2' });\n  },\n\n  stage2class(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'c2', 1040);\n    const cam = s.camera([\n      view(s, g, [260, 1350, 960, 1900], { at: '#sub' }),\n      view(s, g, [400, 1440, 880, 1700], { at: '#poly' }),\n      view(s, g, [650, 1090, 1468, 1680], { at: '#same' }),\n    ]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Stage 2 class diagram');\n      captions(s, [\n        caption('Customer is abstract: Regular, VIP and Critic extend it', 0.8, '#poly'),\n        caption('Waiter and controller call the hooks on Customer, never on a type', '#poly', '#same'),\n        caption('Same order and service flow for every customer', '#same', undefined),\n      ]);\n    });\n  },\n\n  stage2diff(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Stage 1 to Stage 2');\n    term(s, {\n      y: 400,\n      h: 640,\n      size: 24,\n      title: 'git, tag stage-1',\n      out: s.time('#diff') - 0.3,\n      lines: [{ sh: 'git ls-tree -r --name-only stage-1 -- src', at: 0.8, cps: 40 }, { block: T.tree1 }],\n    });\n    const isNew = (l) => /\\|\\s+\\d+\\s+\\++\\s*$/.test(l);\n    const diff = T.diff12;\n    term(s, {\n      y: 430,\n      h: 700,\n      size: 24,\n      title: 'git, stage-1 to stage-2',\n      at: '#diff',\n      lines: [\n        { sh: 'git diff --stat stage-1 stage-2 -- src', cps: 40 },\n        ...diff.map((l, i) => {\n          const last = i === diff.length - 1;\n          const nw = !last && isNew(l);\n          const isOrder = l.includes('entity/Order.java');\n          const isWaiter = l.includes('entity/Waiter.java');\n          const hl = last ? false : nw ? '#new' : isOrder ? '#order' : isWaiter ? '#waiter' : '#mod';\n          const hlc = nw ? 'new' : 'mod';\n          return { out: l, hl, hlc, at: i === 0 ? '#diff' : undefined };\n        }),\n      ],\n    });\n    s.box('5 new files', { x: 520, y: 900, w: 420, size: 44, color: 'green', at: '#new' });\n    s.box('7 modified files', { x: 1040, y: 900, w: 460, size: 44, color: 'yellow', at: '#mod' });\n    s.box('Unchanged: Chef, Table, MenuItem, enums, Money', { x: 1500, y: 980, w: 760, size: 34, color: 'blue', at: '#same' });\n  },\n\n  stage3class(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'c3', 1040);\n    const cam = s.camera([\n      view(s, g, [930, 1290, 1416, 1760], { at: '#combo' }),\n      view(s, g, [1180, 1330, 1416, 1480], { at: '#final' }),\n      view(s, g, [320, 880, 950, 1110], { at: '#hh' }),\n    ]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Stage 3 class diagram');\n      captions(s, [\n        caption('Green: new in Stage 3.  Yellow: changed.  Blue: unchanged.', 0.8, '#combo'),\n        caption('ComboMeal extends MenuItem and holds two MenuItems', '#combo', '#final'),\n        caption('MenuItem is no longer final: the only change it needed', '#final', '#hh'),\n        caption('HappyHour: policy class used by Waiter.takeOrder, locked in Order', '#hh', undefined),\n      ]);\n    });\n  },\n\n  stage3diff(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Stage 2 to Stage 3');\n    const isNew = (l) => /\\|\\s+\\d+\\s+\\++\\s*$/.test(l);\n    const diff = T.diff23;\n    term(s, {\n      y: 430,\n      h: 700,\n      size: 24,\n      title: 'git, stage-2 to stage-3',\n      out: s.time('#lines') - 0.3,\n      lines: [\n        { sh: 'git diff --stat stage-2 stage-3 -- src', at: 0.8, cps: 40 },\n        ...diff.map((l, i) => {\n          const last = i === diff.length - 1;\n          const nw = !last && isNew(l);\n          const clean = /ManagerCommand|StaffActions|GameController/.test(l);\n          return { out: l, hl: last ? false : clean ? '#cleanup' : nw ? 0.6 : false, hlc: clean ? 'mod' : 'new' };\n        }),\n      ],\n    });\n    s.text('GameController.java', { y: 230, size: 60, font: 'mono', at: '#lines' });\n    s.text('324', { x: 620, y: 520, size: 210, font: 'display', color: 'red', at: '#lines' });\n    s.text('lines before the clean-up', { x: 620, y: 700, size: 40, color: 'muted', at: '#lines' });\n    s.arrow([820, 520], [1100, 520], { at: s.time('#lines') + 1.2 });\n    s.text('150', { x: 1300, y: 520, size: 210, font: 'display', color: 'green', at: s.time('#lines') + 1.6 });\n    s.text('lines after', { x: 1300, y: 700, size: 40, color: 'muted', at: s.time('#lines') + 1.6 });\n    s.box('Identical transcripts before and after: no rule changed', { y: 890, w: 1300, size: 48, icon: 'git-compare', color: 'green', at: '#same' });\n  },\n\n  tests(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Evidence that earlier behaviour still works');\n    const out = s.time('#bug') - 0.3;\n    s.text('GameTests: checks passed at each tag', { y: 150, size: 60, font: 'display', at: 0.4, out });\n    const xs = [430, 960, 1490];\n    [[55, 'stage-1', '#t1'], [108, 'stage-2', '#t2'], [194, 'stage-3', '#t3']].forEach(([n, name, at], i) => {\n      s.number(n, { x: xs[i], y: 330, size: 170, color: ['blue', 'green', 'purple'][i], at, dur: 1.0, out });\n      s.text(`${name}, 0 failed`, { x: xs[i], y: 470, size: 42, color: 'muted', at, out });\n    });\n    term(s, {\n      y: 780,\n      h: 330,\n      size: 24,\n      title: 'stage-3 tag',\n      out,\n      lines: [\n        { sh: 'javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java', at: 0.8, cps: 45 },\n        { sh: 'java -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests', cps: 45 },\n        { out: '194 checks passed, 0 failed', c: 'ok', at: '#t3' },\n      ],\n    });\n    s.text('A gap the recorded runs missed', { y: 200, size: 72, font: 'display', at: '#bug' });\n    s.box('SERVE or COOK with no number chose from all customers who had ordered', { y: 400, w: 1500, size: 44, icon: 'bug', color: 'red', at: '#bug' });\n    s.box('Fix: StaffActions uses the same eligibility lists as the screen', { y: 620, w: 1500, size: 44, icon: 'wrench', color: 'green', at: s.cue('fixed') });\n    s.box('5 more checks, including two READY orders', { y: 840, w: 1100, size: 44, icon: 'check', color: 'blue', at: s.cue('five') });\n  },\n\n  principles(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Design principles, with code evidence');\n    const cards = [\n      ['Single responsibility', 'GameController sequences the turn.\\nManagerCommand reads a choice.\\nStaffActions carries it out.', 'blue', '#srp', 520, 340],\n      ['Open and closed', 'Three customer types added without\\ntouching the turn logic. ComboMeal needs\\nno change in Chef, Cashier, Menu, Order.', 'green', '#ocp', 1400, 340],\n      ['Liskov substitution', 'Any Customer subclass works as a Customer.\\nA 3-unit combo works in an ordinary Order\\n(tested).', 'purple', '#lsp', 520, 730],\n      ['Dependency direction', 'RestaurantCLI no longer depends on\\nGameController. A test checks the source.', 'orange', '#dep', 1400, 730],\n    ];\n    cards.forEach(([title, body, color, at, x, y]) => {\n      s.box(title, { x, y: y - 110, w: 760, size: 46, color, at });\n      s.text(body, { x, y: y + 40, size: 36, lineHeight: 1.4, valign: 'center', maxWidth: 760, at });\n    });\n    s.text('No interface was added only to name a principle', { y: 1000, size: 42, color: 'muted', bold: true, at: '#noint' });\n  },\n\n  tradeoffs(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Trade-offs and alternatives');\n    const rows = [\n      ['HappyHour is a concrete class', 'Alternative: a PricingPolicy interface.\\nExtract it when a second promotion appears.', 'blue', '#hh'],\n      ['Bill is locked in the Order', 'Alternative: the Cashier prices at payment.\\nA promotion ending early would change the bill.', 'green', '#lock'],\n      ['ComboMeal extends MenuItem\\nand holds two items', 'Alternative: a plain item with a fixed price.\\nIt would not be a real combination.', 'purple', '#combo'],\n      ['Behaviour lives in Customer\\nsubclasses', 'Alternative: strategy objects.\\nA mixed type, such as a VIP critic, needs a new subclass.', 'orange', '#strategy'],\n    ];\n    rows.forEach(([ours, alt, color, at], i) => {\n      const y = 230 + i * 235;\n      s.box(ours, { x: 560, y, w: 840, h: 160, size: 42, color, at });\n      s.text(alt, { x: 1340, y, size: 36, lineHeight: 1.35, maxWidth: 840, align: 'left', at });\n    });\n  },\n\n  // ===================== 4. Sequence diagram =====================\n  seqcase(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'q1', 1000);\n    const cam = s.camera([view(s, g, [0, 55, 1150, 215], { at: 0.4, area: { w: 1760, h: 420, cy: 280 } })]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Stage 3 sequence diagram: a VIP orders a Burger during Happy Hour');\n      const lines = [\n        ['*Use case.* Stage 3: a VIP orders a Burger while Happy Hour is on', '#vip'],\n        ['*Starts.* Turn 1, empty restaurant, 2 free tables, Happy Hour unused', '#start'],\n        ['*Ends.* Turn 5: the VIP pays $10.80 with satisfaction 95', '#end'],\n        ['*Manager decision.* Happy Hour on or off. Off: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90', '#decision'],\n      ];\n      lines.forEach(([t, at], i) => s.text(t, { x: -840, y: 90 + i * 95, align: 'left', size: 46, maxWidth: 1700, at }));\n    });\n  },\n\n  seqturn1(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'q1', 1000);\n    const cam = s.camera([\n      view(s, g, [380, 390, 1440, 560], { at: '#arr' }),\n      view(s, g, [250, 640, 1350, 810], { at: '#alt' }),\n      view(s, g, [380, 1255, 1640, 1450], { at: '#seat' }),\n      view(s, g, [380, 1560, 1620, 1700], { at: '#loss' }),\n    ]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Sequence diagram, turn 1');\n      captions(s, [\n        caption('Lifelines: Manager, RestaurantCLI (boundary), GameController, StaffActions, ManagerCommand (control), domain objects', 0.6, '#arr', { size: 36 }),\n        caption('isArrivalTurn(1), createArrival(1), registerArrival(vip)', '#arr', '#alt'),\n        caption('alt [choice == y]: activate() sets 2 remaining turns', '#alt', '#seat'),\n        caption('Host WAITs, so the Waiter seats the VIP. Queue entry removed only after acceptance', '#seat', '#loss'),\n        caption('reduceSatisfaction(): the VIP loses 5 (100 to 95)', '#loss', undefined),\n      ]);\n    });\n  },\n\n  seqturn2(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'q2', 1000);\n    const cam = s.camera([\n      view(s, g, [250, 140, 1450, 235], { at: 0.3, dur: 0.5 }),\n      view(s, g, [1050, 395, 1850, 625], { at: '#take' }),\n      view(s, g, [1250, 770, 1950, 905], { at: '#chef' }),\n    ]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Sequence diagram, turn 2');\n      captions(s, [\n        caption('Happy Hour recovery: recoverSatisfaction(15), 95 to 100', 0.6, '#take'),\n        caption('calculateBillCents(1500) = 1350,  applyDiscount(1350) = 1080', '#take', '#lock'),\n        caption('new Order(item, 1080, 2): billCents has no setter, the price stays locked', '#lock', '#chef'),\n        caption('Chef: advancePreparation() makes the order READY. Serving waits for turn 3', '#chef', undefined),\n      ]);\n    });\n  },\n\n  seqturn3(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'q2', 1000);\n    const cam = s.camera([\n      view(s, g, [20, 1140, 1000, 1250], { at: 0.3, dur: 0.5 }),\n      view(s, g, [1050, 1285, 1850, 1490], { at: '#alt' }),\n      view(s, g, [230, 1500, 1100, 1620], { at: '#bad' }),\n    ]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Sequence diagram, turn 3');\n      captions(s, [\n        caption('loop [until the Waiter choice is accepted]', 0.6, '#alt'),\n        caption('alt [a READY customer is selected]: markServed(), onServed(...)', '#alt', '#bad'),\n        caption('Otherwise: exception, displayError(reason), the manager chooses again', '#bad', undefined),\n      ]);\n    });\n  },\n\n  seqpay(s) {\n    const g = dgeom(s, 'q3', 1000);\n    const cam = s.camera([\n      view(s, g, [380, 605, 1500, 700], { at: 0.3, dur: 0.5 }),\n      view(s, g, [700, 695, 1700, 790], { at: '#cant' }),\n      view(s, g, [1050, 985, 1980, 1185], { at: '#cash' }),\n      view(s, g, [10, 1225, 1300, 1350], { at: '#end' }),\n    ]);\n    drawDiagram(s, g);\n    hud(s, cam, () => {\n      tag(s, 'Sequence diagram, turns 4, 5 and the result');\n      captions(s, [\n        caption('Turn 4: canFinishEating(4) is true, markReadyToPay(4)', 0.6, '#cant'),\n        caption('canPay(4) is false (4 > readyTurn 4 does not hold): no charge yet', '#cant', '#cash'),\n        caption('Turn 5: processPayment reads getBillCents() = 1080, recordPayment, markPaid, release()', '#cash', '#end'),\n        caption('After turn 18: hasMetObjective(), alt [won] Victory, [not won] Defeat', '#end', undefined),\n      ]);\n    });\n  },\n\n  seqwhy(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Sequence diagram: how the messages implement the use case');\n    s.box('The controller never checks the customer type', { x: 960, y: 250, w: 1500, h: 130, size: 52, icon: 'git-fork', color: 'blue', at: '#poly' });\n    s.text('The VIP discount and the loss per turn come from overridden methods', { x: 960, y: 370, size: 40, color: 'muted', at: '#poly' });\n    s.box('The bill is worked out once, in the Waiter', { x: 960, y: 560, w: 1500, h: 130, size: 52, icon: 'lock', color: 'green', at: '#once' });\n    s.text('The Cashier only reads getBillCents(), so a promotion ending early changes nothing', { x: 960, y: 680, size: 40, color: 'muted', maxWidth: 1500, at: '#once' });\n    s.box('Recorded run: the Host seats the VIP while the Waiter takes the order', { x: 960, y: 870, w: 1500, h: 130, size: 46, icon: 'zap', color: 'orange', at: '#run' });\n    s.text('So the same VIP pays on turn 4 instead of turn 5', { x: 960, y: 985, size: 40, color: 'muted', at: s.cue('payment') });\n  },\n\n  // ===================== 5. Individual contributions and key learning =====================\n  team(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Individual contributions');\n    const people = [\n      ['Li Mu-En', 'Repository and stage tags, Java implementation, Stage 3 clean-up, reworked diagrams', '#mu', 520, 330],\n      ['Peramudhan', 'Stage 3 sequence diagram: use case and first full draft', '#peram', 1400, 330],\n      ['Ryan', 'Stage 2 and 3 class diagrams: customer subclasses, ComboMeal, SOLID discussion', '#ryan', 520, 720],\n      ['Ye Fei', 'Stage 1 class diagram and consistency review across the stages', '#yefei', 1400, 720],\n    ];\n    people.forEach(([name, role, at, x, y]) => {\n      s.box(name, { x, y: y - 40, w: 760, h: 130, size: 54, icon: 'user-round', color: 'accent', at });\n      s.text(role, { x, y: y + 90, size: 36, maxWidth: 740, lineHeight: 1.35, valign: 'top', at });\n    });\n  },\n\n  learnmuen(s) {\n    learn(s, 'Li Mu-En', 'Ask which object should own each rule. Guards sit next to the state they protect, so an invalid command cannot half change the game.', 'Keeping the code, the diagrams, the README and the three tags consistent.');\n  },\n  learnperam(s) {\n    learn(s, 'Peramudhan', 'Every arrow in a sequence diagram says which object owns an action. The VIP bill showed polymorphism in practice.', 'The diagram went out of date when the clean-up changed the code, so it was redrawn once the code was stable.');\n  },\n  learnryan(s) {\n    learn(s, 'Ryan', 'Inheritance, polymorphism and abstraction support the open and closed principle.', 'Choosing between a new subclass and changing an existing class, and keeping growing diagrams readable.');\n  },\n  learnyefei(s) {\n    learn(s, 'Ye Fei', 'Single responsibility and encapsulation decide where each operation belongs. After feedback, the Stage 1 diagram gained getOrder, servedTurn, readyTurn and releaseTableOf.', 'Telling association, composition and dependency apart.');\n  },\n\n  closing(s) {\n    s.camera([]);\n    sec(s, 'Restaurant Rush');\n    s.text('Restaurant Rush', { y: 190, size: 100, font: 'display', enter: 'none', at: 0 });\n    s.text('github.com/RobotHanzo/SC2002GroupProject', { y: 360, size: 58, font: 'mono', color: 'accent', at: '#repo' });\n    s.text('tags: stage-1   stage-2   stage-3', { y: 470, size: 46, font: 'mono', at: '#repo' });\n    s.text('compare/stage-1...stage-2\\ncompare/stage-2...stage-3', { y: 620, size: 42, font: 'mono', color: 'muted', lineHeight: 1.5, at: '#repo' });\n    s.text('AI chat histories: docs/individual/<member>/chat-history', { y: 780, size: 40, color: 'muted', at: s.cue('conversations') });\n    s.box('Three runnable stages, every change explained', { y: 920, w: 1400, size: 56, icon: 'flag', color: 'green', at: '#end' });\n  },\n};\n\nfunction learn(s, name, learned, hard) {\n  s.camera([]);\n  sec(s, 'Key learning');\n  s.icon('user-round', { x: 330, y: 190, size: 100, color: 'accent', at: 0.3 });\n  s.text(name, { x: 450, y: 190, size: 100, font: 'display', align: 'left', at: 0.3 });\n  s.text('WHAT I LEARNED', { x: 520, y: 360, size: 36, bold: true, color: 'green', at: 1.0 });\n  s.box(learned, { x: 520, y: 640, w: 800, h: 470, size: 42, color: 'green', at: 1.0 });\n  s.text('HARDEST PART', { x: 1400, y: 360, size: 36, bold: true, color: 'orange', at: '#hard' });\n  s.box(hard, { x: 1400, y: 640, w: 800, h: 470, size: 42, color: 'orange', at: '#hard' });\n}\n"
}

// Two-pass loudness normalization to `target` LUFS, peak-limited.
export async function normalizeLoudness(input, output, target = -14, sampleRate = 48000) {
  const base = `I=${target}:TP=-1.5:LRA=11`;
  const pass1 = await ffmpeg(['-i', input, '-af', `loudnorm=${base}:print_format=json`, '-f', 'null', '-']);
  const json = pass1.stderr.slice(pass1.stderr.lastIndexOf('{'), pass1.stderr.lastIndexOf('}') + 1);
  const m = JSON.parse(json);
  // Silence and very short clips have no measurable integrated loudness.
  if (!Number.isFinite(Number(m.input_i))) {
    await ffmpeg(['-i', input, '-ar', String(sampleRate), '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', output]);
    return { inputI: Number(m.input_i), inputTP: Number(m.input_tp) };
  }
  const measured = `measured_I=${m.input_i}:measured_TP=${m.input_tp}:measured_LRA=${m.input_lra}:measured_thresh=${m.input_thresh}:offset=${m.target_offset}`;
  await ffmpeg(['-i', input, '-af', `loudnorm=${base}:${measured}:linear=true:print_format=summary`, '-ar', String(sampleRate), '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', output]);
  return { inputI: Number(m.input_i), inputTP: Number(m.input_tp) };
}

export async function probe(file) {
  const { stdout } = await run(ffprobePath(), ['-v', 'error', '-show_streams', '-show_format', '-of', 'json', file]);
  return JSON.parse(stdout);
}

export async function loudness(file) {
  const { stderr } = await ffmpeg(['-nostats', '-i', file, '-filter_complex', 'ebur128=peak=true', '-f', 'null', '-']);
  const summary = stderr.slice(stderr.lastIndexOf('Summary:'));
  const I = /I:\s*(-?[\d.]+) LUFS/.exec(summary);
  const LRA = /LRA:\s*(-?[\d.]+) LU/.exec(summary);

{
  "file_path": "D:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\docs\\video\\README.md",
  "content": "# Presentation video\n\n`restaurant-rush-demo.mp4` is the group presentation: 15 minutes 19 seconds, 1920 x 1080, with narration.\n`restaurant-rush-demo-small.mp4` is the same video re-encoded to 55 MB (the original is 166 MB, above the 100 MB limit of a normal Git push).\n\n| File | What it is |\n|---|---|\n| `restaurant-rush-demo.mp4` | Full-quality video, chapters inside the file |\n| `restaurant-rush-demo-small.mp4` | Same video, smaller file |\n| `restaurant-rush-demo.srt`, `.vtt` | Subtitles (upload them with the video for captions) |\n| `restaurant-rush-demo.chapters.txt` | Chapters for a YouTube description |\n| `restaurant-rush-demo.cover.jpg` | Cover picture |\n| `source/` | The files the video is built from (see \"Rebuilding\") |\n\n## Timeline\n\n| Time | Part | Suggested length in the briefing |\n|---|---|---|\n| 0:00 | Game overview and the three stages | 2 min |\n| 1:45 | Gameplay demonstration | 4 min |\n| 5:39 | Class diagrams and stage changes | 4 min |\n| 10:19 | Selected use case and sequence diagram | 3 min |\n| 13:08 | Individual contributions and key learning | 2 min |\n\nThe timestamps for the README table of the assignment are in the main [README](../../README.md#video-timestamps).\n\n## What on screen is real\n\n- Every terminal line is the real output of the game and of `git`, captured from the stage tags: the victory and defeat runs of `stage-3`, `git diff --stat` between the tags, `git ls-tree` of `stage-1`, and the result line of `GameTests`.\n  The data is in `source/transcripts.js`. Long lines wrap like a terminal does and the manager's typed input is animated; the text is not edited.\n- The two \"same run, one choice changed\" results (turn 13, Critic 54 or 42, average 81.2 or 78.8) come from replaying `runs/victory.txt` with that one choice changed. Revenue stays $83.70 in both. Happy Hour never switched on gives $86.40 and 80.2, as in `docs/test-notes.md`.\n- The diagrams are the PDFs in `docs/diagrams`, rendered to pictures and shown zoomed in.\n\n## The narration is synthetic\n\nThe voice is Kokoro `af_heart`, produced on this computer by explainroo. The briefing asks each member to explain a part of the work.\n`source/script.md` has `> Presenter from here: <name>` notes that suggest who explains which part, following the roles in the group report.\nTo use real voices, each member records their scenes and the group renders again:\n\n1. Record every scene of `source/script.md` as `assets/narration/<scene-id>.wav` (any of wav, mp3, m4a, aac, flac, ogg, opus). The scene ids are the `## ...` lines.\n2. In `video.json` set `\"voice\": { \"provider\": \"recording\", \"src\": \"assets/narration/\" }`.\n3. Render again. The timeline changes with the recordings, so copy the new times into the README table from `out/video.chapters.txt` and the scene starts in `build/timeline.json`.\n\nThe small `explainroo.com` mark in the corner is explainroo's default watermark. Set `\"watermark\": false` in `video.json` to remove it.\n\n## Rebuilding\n\nThe video is made with [explainroo](https://github.com/vincentsch/explainroo).\n\n```\ngit clone https://github.com/vincentsch/explainroo.git\ncd explainroo\nnpm install\nnode bin/explainroo.js doctor --fetch\nmkdir videos/restaurant-rush\n# copy the contents of docs/video/source/ into videos/restaurant-rush/\nnode bin/explainroo.js voice videos/restaurant-rush\nnode bin/explainroo.js render videos/restaurant-rush\nnode bin/explainroo.js verify videos/restaurant-rush\n```\n\nIt needs Node, ffmpeg and Chrome. The diagram pictures in `source/assets` come from `docs/diagrams/*.pdf` (stage 1, 2 and 3 class diagrams, and the three pages of the Stage 3 sequence diagram).\n"
}
~~~

I've made the demo video with explainroo: 15 minutes 19 seconds at 1920×1080, with synthetic narration. I checked it with explainroo's `verify`: narration understood 99%, loudness −16 LUFS, no warnings. I haven't listened to it end to end.

It follows the briefing's five blocks, in this order:

| Time | Part |
|---|---|
| 0:00 | Game overview and the three stages |
| 1:45 | Gameplay demonstration |
| 5:39 | Class diagrams and stage changes |
| 10:19 | Sequence diagram |
| 13:08 | Individual contributions and key learning |

The gameplay section covers all seven segments listed in the assignment, and the class-diagram section covers the stage-tag comparisons. The scoring rubric is never mentioned or shown; I searched the script, scenes, subtitles and notes for related words and found none.

**The video isn't in members' own voices yet.** The brief says each member should explain a part, and the narration is a single synthetic voice (Kokoro `af_heart`). `docs/video/source/script.md` has `> Presenter from here:` notes suggesting who covers which part, based on the roles in your report. Those assignments are my guess. The recording steps are in `docs/video/README.md`. Re-rendering with recordings changes the timeline, so the README timestamps would need refreshing.

**What's on screen**
- **Terminals:** every terminal line is real output from the game and from git, captured from your stage tags. Long lines wrap like a terminal does. I animated the typing but didn't edit any text.
- **Turn-13 decision:** I replayed `runs/victory.txt` with the Waiter's turn-13 choice changed. The Critic pays at 54 or 42 and the average is 81.2 or 78.8, matching `docs/test-notes.md`.
- **Diagrams:** they are renders of your PDFs, zoomed in.

**Changes in the repo**
- **New `docs/video/`:** it holds the 166 MB MP4, a 55 MB re-encode for sharing (the original is over GitHub's 100 MB push limit), subtitles, chapters, the cover image and a `source/` folder to rebuild the video. Nothing is committed.
- **README:** I filled in the "Video timestamps" table in `README.md`. That edit is also uncommitted.

**Things to decide**
- **Watermark:** the video has explainroo's default `explainroo.com` watermark in the corner. Setting `"watermark": false` in `video.json` removes it, and the video must be re-rendered (about 12 minutes).
- **Pronunciation:** I spelled the member names phonetically for the voice, and Whisper couldn't confirm them. Worth a listen to the `team` and `learn…` scenes.
- **Block lengths:** the class-diagram section runs about 4:40 against the suggested 4:00, and the sequence section about 2:49 against 3:00. The total is on target.
